1,225 setts across 1,134 tartans with 9 stripes, grouped by base-six **band pattern**, then by human-palette **stripe pattern**. Each band pattern is summarised first (popular first), then listed in full below. Each variant shows ΔT — its mean distance (OKLab ΔE) from the base colours — nearest first, with a stripe bar in the nearest human-palette colour names (hover a stripe to read it).
